- 235 -
The Discoveries of Viktor Schauberger

Earth, Water and Air in the Mirror of an Ethical Technology


---


The Austrian Viktor Schauberger (1885-1958) was a forester, natural philosopher and inventor. Schauberger became known today through his "free energy" machines, apparatuses that generate energy without fossil raw materials or atomic fission; but as a nature-connected forester he also developed, above all, numerous methods for agriculture and for handling water, in order to counteract today's rapacious exploitation of nature, which he denounced again and again in drastic words. Schauberger developed a completely new concept of nature, of energy, and ultimately also of consciousness. As a forester he observed nature for decades in order to find out how nature functions, how growth, movement and life arise. He was a person who could understand nature, and through his family he still stood in the very ancient tradition of the Celtic-Germanic culture. He was gifted with visionary sight, and so could learn the secrets of water and of nature. His understanding of nature and technology differed fundamentally from that of our present highly industrial civilization. Our present culture rests on a technological development that destroys nature and takes away people's living space. Schauberger's nature-close biotechnology works exactly the other way around: instead of exhaust gases it produces healing water and pure, fresh air; instead of simply burning valuable raw materials like oil, wood or coal, it produces organic substances, respectively energy values, that enliven the surroundings and improve the quality of the vegetation; instead of the resistance to movement that grows with the square of the acceleration, his bio-movement calls forth an increase in performance that grows with the square of the acceleration. According to Schauberger, old prehistoric cultures knew about these connections and were able, with the least expenditure, to bring forth fabulous harvest yields, to erect buildings like the pyramids and the old gigantic temples, and altogether to create paradisiacal conditions. Unfortunately this knowledge has been lost for various reasons. In the course of his researches Schauberger developed novel methods and techniques in the most diverse fields: in agriculture, in composting, water refinement and forest care, in the construction of home power plants, lighting and heating installations, as well as of aircraft and ships. At the same time his work is highly interesting for questions of philosophy.

- 236 -
Living Water


---


Water was the most important element for Viktor Schauberger. The flow forms of water with its vortices, meanders, whirlpools, spirals and wave movements are the expression of the life-building movement principle discovered by Schauberger, which is fundamentally to be distinguished from the life-dissolving type of movement. In this sense, the life-building movement was, for Schauberger, a nature-correct movement, one that corresponds to nature. Today's technology uses the life-dissolving movement, which in nature is used to break down organic residues, for building up the economy. In this sense, the technical methods of movement (combustion engine, propeller, screw, turbine, among others) are nature-contrary movements that harm nature and life. Schauberger asked himself again and again why springs rise on the peaks of mountains, when according to gravity the water ought to be found at the lowest point. He was also fascinated by the phenomenon of the standing trout. In rushing mountain streams one can observe trout that stand motionless in the strong current and let food swim into their mouths. If one startles them, they do not flee downstream, but dart away upstream with unbelievable speed. What force is at work here? After decades of observing and empathizing, the right idea came to Schauberger. There had to be a buoyant force that functions as the polar counterpart to gravity, to gravitation: levitation. Schauberger recognized that this force is everywhere — in water, in plants, in animals and in human beings. It is the force that enables water to rise, and that makes life possible for us humans. The life-building movement is a spiral-shaped, oscillating, pulling movement, whereas the life-dissolving, decomposing movement is a sluggish, straight-line, pushing movement. The decomposing movement is an important component of nature when it comes to dissolving dead organic substances such as leaves or animal carcasses and returning them to the natural cycle. Far more important, however, is the building-up levitation energy, practically unresearched today, which brings forth growth, freedom of movement, the orientation of living beings and, ultimately, also the formation of the mind. In this context Schauberger distinguishes between two fundamental polar partial forces of movement that complement each other: pressure and suction. Levitation is a suction energy that draws its force from the fine-matter, metaphysical realms — that is, realms that are not materially perceptible or measurable. He designates these realms as the origin of the life energy, which manifests itself as organic matter, yet is more than this. Cell formation is based on the suction principle, just as our breathing is


---


- 237 -

...and our blood circulation. The entire vegetation rests upon it, and thus the correct application of the suction energies is indispensable if one wishes to cultivate the gifts of nature, the plants and fruits, in the right way. The manner in which we move water in particular is vital to life, for water is the foundation of life. The same principle applies, however, also to the earth and to the air.

"In nature there are no straight lines."

This pronouncement of Schauberger's is programmatic. Today's water management always conducts water in straightened forms; riverbeds are laid out just as straight as household water pipes. In nature, however, one can observe that water always seeks its way in meandering form, that is, in serpentine lines, and in this movement continually spirals in on itself. According to Schauberger this is the correct flow form of water, in which it can energetically charge itself and become refreshed and ennobled. If one forces water to flow in straight pipes instead of in spiral pipes, it loses its carrying and dragging force, becomes stale, and slowly dies. Schauberger called water "the blood of the earth." For him it was an "inorganic organism," a "living being," which in turn is part of a larger living being, the earth. Just as the blood flows in our human body, cleansing the body and supplying it with nutrients, so does water do this for the body of the earth. Water is the carrier of the life force. It springs from the tops of the mountains in bubbling springs, and everyone knows that high-spring water is the purest and healthiest water. After maturing and being ennobled for a long time in the interior of the earth, sealed off from air and light, it comes to the surface of the earth when it is ready to do so. The quality of the water increases with every stone around which it flows, with every curve in which it can oscillate, and it radiates fine-matter energies out to its surroundings everywhere, to nourish the vegetation. Water flowing in a nature-correct manner does not only move in meandering form, does not only need the stones of impetus, but is also always in the shade. Trees and shrubs line the bank and give the water the life-giving shade it needs in order to preserve its quality, which springs from a specific charge. Water exposed to the sun becomes aggressive and destroys the banks. Shaded water becomes cool, thereby approaches its greatest specific density at +4°C, and, on account of the rolling-in at the axis center, moves fastest, while the finest organic suspended matter — energy-substance carriers of the highest quality, organic residues and trace elements — are washed out at the bank's edge, can deposit themselves there, and thereby supply the surroundings with new building substances. Especially the cooling effect, which is not only brought about by the shade but actually arises primarily from the


---


- 238 -

...spiral movement, has, for Schauberger, the greatest significance. The anomaly point of water at +4°C is, to him, the qualitatively best state of water, because here it has the highest specific density, and thus the greatest carrying and dragging force, and is thereby able to carry along the sediment loads such as sand and gravel, which in turn, through constant mechanical abrasion, give off their trace elements and organic inclusions to the water. In this sense Schauberger calls the sediment loads the provisions of the water, which are lost when the water is straightened and overheated. Mature water — and thus water suitable for use — is, according to Schauberger, only water that comes out of the earth by itself. Deep wells are therefore not suitable, because the water is not yet mature, and rainwater is likewise not ideal, because it has no geospheric tension. Good springs can be recognized by the fact that they flow equally strongly in winter as in summer, regardless of whether drought or heavy precipitation prevails.

Log-Fluming Installations

How important the correct flow form and temperature of water is, Schauberger demonstrated with resounding success in his log-fluming installations. Log flumes serve in the mountains to transport tree trunks from the high forests down into the valley. Normally these are laid out as straight as possible, in order to exploit the optimal gradient. Schauberger, however, did not lay out the flumes in straight lines, but built them along the small mountain streams that meandered in serpentine fashion down into the valley. The experts were convinced that such an installation could not work at all. The first attempt, however, produced an overwhelming success, for the quantity of wood transported increased many times over.

What had happened?

Schauberger had constructed the channel of the flume with an egg-shaped cross-section, laid it out in meandering curves, and additionally fitted it with small "fins" that brought the water into the correct rolling-in. Furthermore, fresh, cold water was fed in every few hundred meters. All of these components led to the water concentrating in the middle of the channel and forming a spiral suction there, which flushed the logs down very quickly and without their touching the walls. Since the logs no longer jammed and the flow velocity in the center of the suction was enormously high, the masses of wood went down the valley at great speed, without being damaged. Through the spiral movement of the water and

- 239 -

with the help of the additional feeding-in of fresh cold water, Schauberger achieved the transport of even logs whose specific weight is heavier than that of water.

The Levitation Energy


---


Beyond the cooling effect, however, the spiral movement brings about yet another effect that is completely unknown in today's science: levitation. It is the levitation energy, the life energy, that causes the trees to grow upward, that enables the bird to fly and the standing trout to stand motionless in the rushing mountain stream. The beating of bird wings, the swaying of trees in the wind, the dance and the play of human beings are nature-correct movements that bring about the build-up of levitation energy. This is a matter of a qualitative substance build-up, of gaining high-grade substances such as healing water, air and earth, and making them available to living beings. Levitation energy springs from a specific movement. Every movement produces molecular or atomic changes of oscillation. Depending on the type of movement, building-up (uplifting) or breaking-down kinds of energy arise. The explanation of levitation leads into chemical and physical realms, but also into fine-matter regions beyond our three-dimensional, perceptible reality.

Nature-correctly flowing water generates levitation energy

Levitation results from implosion. Implosion must be understood as the counterpart to explosion. It acts in a contracting and structure-forming way. Cell formation, for example, and thus all growth, rests upon implosion. Explosion acts in an expanding and structure-dissolving way. Implosion is a voltage gradient that arises through bipolar structures, between which a diffusion resistance (skin, bark, etc.) is located. The bipolar elements are oxygen and carbon. At certain temperatures (between +4° and +9°C) and with exclusion of light and heat, a higher-grade fermentation process takes place, in which not an oxidation but a reduction occurs. The carbon consumes the oxygen. Schauberger writes on this: "We are thus dealing with fundamentally different fermentation processes, which, temperature-controlled, beget the development-promoting or development-inhibiting kinds of energy, whereby in the first case


---


- 240 -
to the escape of the amniotic fluid, in the second case to the decomposition and
putrefaction of the physical firstborn (water), there occurs a joint emission of
emanation matter, which decomposes surrounding groundwater in exactly the
same way as, for example, electric current does, after which — following the
separation of the bipolar fundamental matter values — detonating-gas-like
explosive effects occur, in the second case the so-called
implosion occurs, which acts in a cell-forming manner.« (Viktor Schauberger, Die
Entstehung des Wassers [The Origin of Water], in: Implosion No. 116, 7) The
development-promoting fermentation process leads to the emergence of water —
Schauberger calls it the amniotic fluid of the Earth — while the
development-inhibiting fermentation process leads to putrefaction. These
energies radiate out into the surroundings and act there either
in a building-up or a decomposing way. Nature-correct and building-up technology
must therefore take the path of implosion and not the path of explosion, which
our technology of today pursues with its explosion and fire technology.
»It should be mentioned in advance here that the levitation current build-up,
which takes place through implosion, possesses biomagnetic, that is,
contracting functional forces, and — exactly like terrestrial magnetism — flows
along the longitudinal axis, that is, in nature-correctly flowing
water it flows counter to the direction of flow, toward the origin of the water,
the spring, and takes into its tow, for example, the trout standing in this
higher-grade energy-matter concentration. Through its
mobile gill systems this fish is able to steer these levitation forces in such a way
that it can either stand motionless in the rushing spring water,
dart upstream, or hover aloft in the midst of freely plunging
masses of water, when these falling water masses —
owing to suction curves formed in front of the waterfall — are able to move around
themselves and within themselves, about their own axis, in cycloidal spiral curves,
whereby here too the ideal (soul-like) axis can come into being,
from which — as in our bloodstream — the previously unresearched
emanation values branch out, which animate the body,
whereby it can effortlessly overcome its body weight, insofar as this is
necessary for its free mode of movement, provided
that its blood can maintain the species-specific anomalous states, the indifference
and with it freedom from fever — in other words,
provided it is healthy and thus capable of propagation and reproduction.« (ibid.) The
core of levitation energy is therefore the »soul-like axial center« arising from the
cycloidal spiral curve of the water. In this
center of the spiral, energies form that can no longer be understood
mechanistically and that Schauberger designates as soul-like energy.
It is the energy that gives us life and enables us
to move our body effortlessly against gravity. Precisely
this form of energy, which Schauberger deciphered through decades of
observation of the aforementioned standing trout, he was ultimately
able to copy mechanically. He emphasized again and again that this


---


- 241 -
biotechnology is fundamentally very easy to handle once
one has understood the principle. Unfortunately, for patent-law
reasons he never made the actual key information public and ultimately took it
with him to the grave. Nevertheless, today there exists the possibility of rediscovering
the principle, on the one hand through the rich body of writings that Schauberger
left behind, on the other hand through other researchers who are on the same
trail.

Schauberger's technical developments

Building upon his observations of the flow behavior of water,
Schauberger gradually developed various machines that made
use of this spiraling principle. Inside an air- and light-tight housing he brought
either water or air into the
centripetal, inward-spiraling direction of movement, by guiding it over so-called
meander discs (two discs lying upon one another that have the
wave profile of a water surface onto which a drop of water
falls) and through spiral spiral pipes of oval, egg-shaped cross-section.
After an externally applied impulse, the medium
(water or air) moved in a circuit through this apparatus which — once set
in motion — became ever faster without any further supply of energy or fuel,
and could be harnessed for energy generation via turbine wheels. Through the
nature-correct flowing movement of the medium,
no frictional resistance arose. The centripetal rolling-in moreover brought it about
that the pressure resistance finally became negative, that is, the medium
acquired drawing (suctional) properties. Beyond this he developed devices for
the ennoblement of water and achieved such great successes with them that he
became known throughout Austria as a healer, and was visited daily by numerous
sick and needy people who were healed after drinking the water. In the fields of
forestry and agriculture Schauberger
demonstrated with the construction of bio-ploughs and garden-cultivation implements made of
bronze entirely novel concepts, in order to increase the yields and the quality of
the harvests and to arrive at a nature-correct treatment of nature. He pointed out
the connection between forest and water and
warned again and again against treating the forest and the field with the previous
nature-hostile methods, which lead to the dying-off of the higher
plant and animal world and to the disappearance of the water.
The increasing karstification of whole regions worldwide as well as the sinking
of the groundwater in otherwise fertile zones are the visible
symptoms of the nature-hostile technology of our day. »Times will
come in which a glass of pure water will cost more than a glass of
wine,« Schauberger commented.

- 242 -
Pressure and suction


---


Schauberger's master teacher was nature. Growth, movement,
ennoblement, progress are the genuine characteristics of nature. Nature
and life function in a harmonious and life-promoting way
without producing poison, noise, and filth. Today's technology,
by contrast, works according to the opposite principle. The nature-correct
mode of movement (displacement) functions according
to the suction principle: without resistance, free of pressure and heat, implosive,
structure-diminishing, attractive, and centripetal. This energy works with organic
fatty substances and higher-grade atomic energies, which act in a levitating — that is,
buoyant and hence building-up — manner, and are connected with physically
imperceptible forms of radiation. The nature-incorrect
mode of energy movement works according to the pressure principle: with resistance,
pressure- and heat-generating, explosive, structure-enlarging, repulsive,
and centrifugal. It acts gravitatingly, hence recoilingly and decomposingly.
In nature both energies exist, and there they each fulfill their purpose.
If, however, one wishes to use in technology and science the degrading
pressure energies in order to improve or
secure human life, this leads to constant destruction and
loss of quality. In order to generate energy or movement, it is therefore
nature-correct to use the building-up, levitating suction forces, which are released
by a suitable movement impulse. Then it becomes
possible to copy the course of the water and to contribute to the healing of the Earth
and of humanity. According to Schauberger, these two
different approaches also have effects on the surroundings
and the substances with which they have to do. The movement expresses itself in the
molecules and changes their oscillation. From this arise
radiations of an atomic kind, which act on the surroundings either in a building-up
or a degrading way. Pressure turbines decompose the water and lead to
a loss of quality; suction turbines, such as Schauberger developed,
build up the water — that is, they produce healing water. Centrifugal ploughs,
such as are used in agriculture today, discharge and
decompose the soil; centripetal, inward-rolling ploughs after Schauberger's design,
which moreover must be made of beryllium-copper (bronze) and not of iron,
ennoble the soil and lead to clear increases in yield
and quality. It is vital to preserve the water, the soil, and
the air, for otherwise the decline of
human civilization will advance ceaselessly. With nature-conform technology,
humanity could attain not only freedom of nourishment and
of movement — that is, luxuriant plant abundance and machine power free of
charge — but also a spiritual elevation that


---


- 243 -
connects us once more with the higher levels of life. All of this would lead to a
profound transformation of society, in which the firmly established
hierarchies of rule would no longer endure. With the disappearance of the struggle
for survival, the scarcity of raw materials, and slave labor, those in power would
lack the very means with which they hold the population in dependency and under
control. It was not by chance that Schauberger had to struggle his whole life with
institutions and persons who sought to take possession of his knowledge for
dishonest reasons. Not by chance was this knowledge suppressed in the
early history of humanity and replaced by nature-hostile
doctrines. Only when many people join in the work and devote themselves to
this natural way of energy generation, the »free energy«, can humanity be restored
to the position appropriate to it: a community of free, happy individuals not driven
by need and misery.
